Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any cycles module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 653. The projective resolution has length at most 388 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to cycles theory, we decompose the problem into 653 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 388. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
